# Mob spawns by biome (generated)

Generated by `tools/gen_spawn_table.py` from `kubejs/exported/spawns.json` (/dumpspawns, 2026-10-10T02:58:57.234771804Z) with `mob_spawns.json` applied. Do not edit by hand: rerun after a dump or after editing `kubejs/generation_scripts/worldgen/mob_spawns.json`. Reference: [mob_spawns.md](mob_spawns.md).

**Reading the numbers:**
- **Share** = weight / total weight of the category in that biome: the chance a spawn attempt of that category there picks this mob.
- **World share** = share weighted by biome area (`biome_config.json`): the part of the category's spawn picks across the region that go to this mob, assuming equal attempts per area. Overworld = surface biomes, Caves = overworld cave biomes, Nether and End = their whole dimension. Not spawns per hour: light, ground blocks and mob caps still decide (section 6).
- Only biome spawner lists. Structure spawns (fortresses, monuments, huts, outposts) and separate spawners (patrols, phantoms, traders, villages) are not in them; section 7 names the known ones.
- Biomes in no dimension (unused or dimension-less) are left out. Area `-` = no sampled area (sky dimensions, pocket dimensions); `<0.03` = under the climate sample's resolution.

## 6. Spawn conditions

The placement and rule a mob must pass after it is picked, for every mob with a spawner, a placement or a spawn egg. Placement: ON_GROUND, IN_WATER, IN_LAVA or NO_RESTRICTIONS. Vanilla rules are named from the bytecode (`tools/spawn_rules_vanilla.json`); a mod's rule shows the class that registered it and the spawn-rule methods on its own classes. "Own checks" = the mob overrides `checkSpawnRules` / `checkSpawnObstruction` (run after the rule). Natural spawns also need the mob cap not to be full, no player within 24 blocks, and space for the mob.
